2006-يىلى ئىيۇلدا، ھېزبوﷲنىڭ ئىسرائىلىيەنىڭ شىمالىي چېگرا جەمئىيەتلىرىگە قىلغان توپ ھۇجۇمى ۋە ئىككى ئىسرائىل ئەسكىرىنىڭ [چېگرا ئارا ئولجىغا ئېلىنىشى](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2006_Hezbollah_cross-border_raid "2006 Hezbollah cross-border raid") ئىسرائىلىيەنىڭ لىۋانغا بېسىۋېلىشىنى ئۆز ئىچىگە ئالغان بىر ئايلىق [ئىككىنچى لىۋان ئۇرۇشىنى](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2006_Lebanon_War "2006 Lebanon War") كەلتۈرۈپ چىقاردى.[^289] [^290] [بىرلەشمە دۆلەتلەر تەشكىلاتى بىخەتەرلىك كېڭىشى 1701-نومۇرلۇق قارارى](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_1701 "United Nations Security Council Resolution 1701") قوبۇل قىلىنغاندىن كېيىن ئۇرۇش 2006-يىلى ئاۋغۇستتا ئاجىزلىدى؛ ئىسرائىل كۈچلىرى كۆپىنچە 2006-يىلى ئۆكتەبىرگە بارغاندا لىۋاندىن چېكىندى، ئەمما [غاجار](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ghajar "Ghajar") كەنتىنىڭ لىۋان قىسمىنى ئىشغال قىلىشنى داۋاملاشتۇردى.[^291] [^292] 2007-يىلى ئىسرائىل ھاۋا كۈچلىرى سۇرىيەدە [بىر يادرو رېئاكتورىنى ۋەيران قىلدى](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Operation_Outside_the_Box "Operation Outside the Box").

2008-يىلى، [ھاماس](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hamas "Hamas") بىلەن ئىسرائىلىيە ئارىسىدىكى [بىر ئۇرۇش توختىتىش](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2008_Israel%E2%80%93Hamas_ceasefire "2008 Israel–Hamas ceasefire") يىمىرىلىپ، ئۈچ ھەپتىلىك [غەزە ئۇرۇشىغا](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gaza_War_\(2008%E2%80%932009\) "Gaza War (2008–2009)") ئېلىپ كەلدى.[^293] [^294] ئىسرائىلىيە جەنۇبىي ئىسرائىل شەھەرلىرىگە قىلىنغان [يۈز دىن ئاشقان پەلەستىن راكېتا ھۇجۇملىرىغا](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_Palestinian_rocket_attacks_on_Israel_in_2012 "List of Palestinian rocket attacks on Israel in 2012") جاۋاب دەپ تەسۋىرلىگەن ھالدا[^295] 2012-يىلى سەككىز كۈن داۋام قىلغان [غەزە تىزمىسىدىكى بىر ئوپېراتسىيىنى](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2012_Gaza_War "2012 Gaza War") باشلىدى.[^296] ئىسرائىلىيە 2014-يىلى ئىيۇلدا ھاماسنىڭ راكېتا ھۇجۇملىرىنىڭ كۈچىيىشىدىن كېيىن [غەزەدىكى يەنە بىر ئوپېراتسىيىنى](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2014_Gaza_War "2014 Gaza War") باشلىدى.[^297] 2021-يىلى مايدا، غەزە ۋە ئىسرائىلىيەدە ئون بىر كۈن داۋام قىلغان يەنە بىر [ئۇرۇش مەيدانى](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2021_Israel%E2%80%93Palestine_crisis "2021 Israel–Palestine crisis") يۈز بەردى.[^298] 2010-يىللارغا بارغاندا، ئىسرائىلىيە بىلەن [ئەرەب بىرلىكى](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Arab_League "Arab League") دۆلەتلىرى ئارىسىدا [كۆپىيىۋاتقان رايونىي ھەمكارلىق](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Arab%E2%80%93Israeli_alliance "Arab–Israeli alliance") قۇرۇلۇپ، [ئىبراھىم كېلىشىملىرىنىڭ](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Abraham_Accords "Abraham Accords") ئىمزالىنىشى بىلەن ئەڭ يۇقىرى چېكىگە يەتتى. ئىسرائىل بىخەتەرلىك ئەھۋالى ئەنئەنىۋى [ئەرەب-ئىسرائىل توقۇنۇشىدىن](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Arab%E2%80%93Israeli_conflict "Arab–Israeli conflict") [ئىران-ئىسرائىل ۋاكالەتچى توقۇنۇشىغا](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Iran%E2%80%93Israel_proxy_conflict "Iran–Israel proxy conflict") ۋە [سۇرىيە پۇقرالىق ئۇرۇشى جەريانىدىكى ئىران بىلەن بىۋاسىتە قارشى تۇرۇشقا](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Iran%E2%80%93Israel_conflict_during_the_Syrian_civil_war "Iran–Israel conflict during the Syrian civil war") يۆتكەلدى.

![](https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/7/73/Fires_in_Israel_and_the_Gaza_strip_-_7_October_2023_%2853245908850%29.jpg/250px-Fires_in_Israel_and_the_Gaza_strip_-_7_October_2023_%2853245908850%29.jpg)

2023-يىلى 7-ئۆكتەبىردە غەزە ئەتراپىدىكى ئوتلارنىڭ سۈنئىي ھەمراھ كۆرۈنۈشى

2023-يىلى 7-ئۆكتەبىردە، [غەزەدىكى](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gaza_Strip "Gaza Strip") ھاماس رەھبەرلىكىدىكى پەلەستىن ھەربىي گۇرۇھلىرى ئىسرائىلىيەگە [بىر قاتار ماسلاشتۇرۇلغان ھۇجۇملارنى](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/October_7_attacks "October 7 attacks") قوزغىدى، بۇ [غەزە ئۇرۇشىنىڭ](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gaza_war "Gaza war") باشلىنىشىغا ئېلىپ كەلدى.[^299] شۇ كۈنى، تەخمىنەن 1,300 ئىسرائىللىق، ئاساسەن پۇقرا، غەزە تىزمىسى چېگراسى يېقىنىدىكى جەمئىيەتلەردە ۋە [بىر مۇزىكا فېستىۋالى جەريانىدا](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Re%27im_music_festival_massacre "Re'im music festival massacre") ئۆلتۈرۈلدى. [200 دىن ئاشقان گىروم](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gaza_war_hostage_crisis "Gaza war hostage crisis") ئولجىغا ئېلىنىپ غەزە تىزمىسىغا ئېلىپ كېتىلدى.[^300] [^301] [^302] سادىقەت تەسىرىنى مودېللاشتۇرۇپ روھىي ساغلاملىق نەتىجىلىرىنى باھالىغان تەتقىقاتلار تەخمىنەن ئىسرائىللىقلارنىڭ %5.3ىنىڭ [PTSD](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/PTSD "PTSD") يېتىلدۈرۈشى مۇمكىنلىكىنى مۆلچەرلىدى،[^303] مىللىي سانلىق مەلۇماتلار ئېھتىمالغا قارشى PTSD نىسبىتىنىڭ %16.2 دىن %29.8 گە دېگەرلىك ئىككى ھەسسە ئاشقانلىقىنى ۋە ھەۋەس-پاراڭ ۋە ئەپسۇسلىنىش نىسبەتلىرىنىڭمۇ كېسكىن ئۆسكەنلىكىنى كۆرسەتتى.[^304]

ئۆز زېمىنىدىن ھەربىي گۇرۇھ ئەزالىرىنى تازىلىغاندىن كېيىن، ئىسرائىلىيە [زامانىۋى تارىختىكى ئەڭ ۋەيران قىلغۇچ بومباردىمان يۈرۈشلىرىدىن بىرىنى](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Israeli_bombing_of_the_Gaza_Strip "Israeli bombing of the Gaza Strip") قوزغىدى [^305] [^306] ۋە ھاماسنى ۋەيران قىلىش ۋە گىروملارنى ئازاد قىلىش دېكلانگەن نىشانلىرى بىلەن 27-ئۆكتەبىردە [غەزەگە بېسىۋالدى](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Israeli_invasion_of_the_Gaza_Strip "Israeli invasion of the Gaza Strip").[^307] [^308] 2008-يىلدىن بېرى [غەزە-ئىسرائىل توقۇنۇشىنىڭ](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gaza%E2%80%93Israel_conflict "Gaza–Israel conflict") بەشىنچى ئۇرۇشى بولغان بۇ ئۇرۇش پۈتۈن [ئىسرائىل-پەلەستىن توقۇنۇشى](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Israeli%E2%80%93Palestinian_conflict "Israeli–Palestinian conflict") ئىچىدە پەلەستىنلىكلەر ئۈچۈن ئەڭ قانلىقى [^309] ۋە 1973-يىلدىكى [يوم كىپپۇر ئۇرۇشىدىن](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Yom_Kippur_War "Yom Kippur War") بېرى رايوننىڭ ئەڭ مۇھىم ھەربىي مەشغۇلاتى بولۇپ قالدى.[^310] بىر [بىرلەشمە دۆلەتلەر تەشكىلاتى ئالاھىدە كومىتېتى](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Special_Committee_to_Investigate_Israeli_Practices_Affecting_the_Human_Rights_of_the_Palestinian_People "Special Committee to Investigate Israeli Practices Affecting the Human Rights of the Palestinian People")، [بىر قانچە ھۆكۈمەت](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/South_Africa%27s_genocide_case_against_Israel "South Africa's genocide case against Israel") ۋە تۈرلۈك مۇتەخەسسىسلەر ۋە ئىنسان ھوقۇقى تەشكىلاتلىرى [غەزە ئۇرۇشى](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gaza_war "Gaza war") جەريانىدا پۇقرالارغا يەتكۈزۈلگەن زىيان ۋە ھايات يوقىتىشلار سەۋەبىدىن ئىسرائىلىيەنىڭ [پەلەستىن خەلقىگە قارشى گېنوسىد سادىر قىلىۋاتقانلىقى](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gaza_genocide "Gaza genocide") دەپ خۇلاسە چىقاردى.[^311] [^312] [^313]

2024-يىلى ئاپرېلدا، ئىران زەربىسى ئىسرائىلىيەنى نىشان قىلغاندىن كېيىن، ئىسرائىلىيە ئىرانغا بىر دولقۇن ھاۋا ھۇجۇملىرىنى باشلىدى، بۇ [2024-يىللىق ئىران-ئىسرائىل توقۇنۇشىنى](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2024_Iran%E2%80%93Israel_conflict "2024 Iran–Israel conflict") ئىككى دۆلەتنىڭ ھەرگىز بىۋاسىتە ئاتىشىپ كۆرگەن تۇنجى ۋەقەسى قىلدى. 2024-يىلى ئۆكتەبىردە، ئىسرائىلىيە [لىۋانغا بېسىۋالدى](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2024_Israeli_invasion_of_Lebanon "2024 Israeli invasion of Lebanon") [^314] ۋە شۇ ئاينىڭ باشلىرىدىكى ئىران زەربىلىرىگە جاۋابەن ئۈچ ھەپتىدىن كېيىن ئىران بىلەن راكېتا ئالماشتۇردى.[^315] ھاماسنى قوللاش ئۈچۈن ھېزبوﷲنىڭ ئىسرائىلىيەگە راكېتا ئېتىشى سەۋەبىدىن 2023-يىلى ئۆكتەبىردىن باشلانغان [ئىسرائىل-ھېزبوﷲ توقۇنۇشىدىن](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Israel%E2%80%93Hezbollah_conflict_\(2023%E2%80%93present\) "Israel–Hezbollah conflict (2023–present)") دېگەرلىك بىر يىل ئۆتكەندىن كېيىن، ئىسرائىلىيە 2024-يىلى سېنتەبىردە ھېزبوﷲنىڭ باش كاتىبى [ھەسەن نەسرۇﷲنى](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hassan_Nasrallah "Hassan Nasrallah") [سۇىقەست قىلدى](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2024_Hezbollah_headquarters_strike "2024 Hezbollah headquarters strike").[^316] 2024-يىلى نويابىردىكى بىر [ئۇرۇش توختىتىش كېلىشىمى](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2024_Israel%E2%80%93Lebanon_ceasefire_agreement "2024 Israel–Lebanon ceasefire agreement") ئىسرائىلىيەنى لىۋاندىن چېكىنىشكە بۇيرۇدى، ئىسرائىلىيە بۇنى 2025-يىلى فېۋرالغا بارغاندا كۆپىنچە قىلدى، ئەمما كېلىشىمگە خىلاپ، ئىسرائىل كۈچلىرى جەنۇبىي لىۋاندىكى ئېگىزلىكلەردە بەش ھەربىي ئالدىنقى پونكىتتا تۇرۇپ قالدى.[^317] [^318] 2025-يىلى ئىيۇندا، ئىسرائىلىيە ئىراننىڭ ھاۋا مۇداپىئە سىستېمىلىرىنى، راكېتا قويۇپ بېرىش قۇرۇلمىلىرىنى، ئۇلارنىڭ ھەربىي رەھبەرلىكىنى ۋە [يادرو پروگراممىسىنى](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nuclear_programme_of_Iran "Nuclear programme of Iran") نىشان قىلغان، [تولۇق كۆلەملىك ئۇرۇشقا](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Twelve-Day_War "Twelve-Day War") ئېشىپ كەتكەن ئىرانغا قارشى يېڭى بىر دولقۇن ھاۋا ھۇجۇملىرىنى باشلىدى.[^319] 2026-يىلى فېۋرالدا، ئىسرائىلىيە ۋە ئامېرىكا قوشما شتاتلىرى ئىرانغا كەڭ كۆلەملىك ھاۋا ھۇجۇملىرىنى قوزغىپ، ئىران ئالىي رەھبىرى [ئەلى خامەنەئىنى](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ali_Khamenei "Ali Khamenei") [سۇىقەست قىلىپ](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Assassination_of_Ali_Khamenei "Assassination of Ali Khamenei") [2026-يىللىق ئىران ئۇرۇشىنى](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2026_Iran_war "2026 Iran war") ۋە [2026-يىللىق لىۋان ئۇرۇشىنى](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2026_Lebanon_war "2026 Lebanon war") قوزغىدى. ھۇجۇملار ئىراننىڭ ھەربىي كۈچىنى، يادرو پروگراممىسىنى ۋەيران قىلىش ۋە [رېجىم ئالماشتۇرۇشقا ئېرىشىشنى](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Regime_change_efforts_in_the_2026_Iran_war "Regime change efforts in the 2026 Iran war") نىشان قىلدى.[^320]

![](https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/c/c9/Satellite_image_of_Israel_in_January_2003.jpg/120px-Satellite_image_of_Israel_in_January_2003.jpg) ![](https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/6/60/Israel_at_night.jpg/120px-Israel_at_night.jpg)

كۈندۈز ۋە كېچىدە ئىسرائىلىيە ۋە قوشنا زېمىنلارنىڭ [سۈنئىي ھەمراھ رەسىملىرى](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Satellite_imagery "Satellite imagery")

ئىسرائىلىيە [مۇنبەت ئاي ئەگىشى](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Fertile_Crescent "Fertile Crescent") نىڭ [لېۋانت](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Levant "Levant") رايونىدا جايلاشقان. [ئوتتۇرا دېڭىزنىڭ](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mediterranean_Sea "Mediterranean Sea") [شەرقىي ئۇچىدا](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Eastern_Mediterranean "Eastern Mediterranean")، ئۇ شىمالدا لىۋان، شىمال-شەرقتە سۇرىيە، شەرقتە ئىئوردانىيە ۋە غەربىي قىرغاق، جەنۇب-غەربتە مىسىر ۋە غەزە تىزمىسى بىلەن چېگرىلىنىدۇ. ئۇ [29°](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/29th_parallel_north "29th parallel north") بىلەن [34° شىمالىي كەڭلىك](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/34th_parallel_north "34th parallel north")، ۋە [34°](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/34th_meridian_east "34th meridian east") بىلەن [36° شەرقىي ئۇزۇنلۇق](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/36th_meridian_east "36th meridian east") ئارىسىدا ياتىدۇ.

ئىسرائىلىيەنىڭ ئىگىلىك ھوقۇقلۇق زېمىنى ([1949-يىللىق ئۇرۇش توختىتىش كېلىشىملىرىنىڭ](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/1949_Armistice_Agreements "1949 Armistice Agreements") چېگرا سىزىقلىرى بويىچە ۋە ئىسرائىلىيە 1967-يىللىق ئالتە كۈنلۈك ئۇرۇشتا قولغا ئالغان بارلىق زېمىنلارنى چىقىرىۋەتكەندە) تەخمىنەن 20,770 كىۋادرات كىلومېتىر (8,019 كىۋادرات ميل) بولۇپ، ئۇنىڭ ئىككى پىروسينتى سۇدۇر.[^321] ئەمما ئىسرائىلىيە شۇنچە تار (ئەڭ كەڭ جايىدا 100 km، شىمالدىن جەنۇبقا 400 km بىلەن سېلىشتۇرغاندا) كى، ئوتتۇرا دېڭىزدىكى [ئىقتىسادىي مەخسۇس رايون](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Exclusive_economic_zone "Exclusive economic zone") دۆلەتنىڭ قۇرۇقلۇق كۆلىمىدىن ئىككى ھەسسە چوڭ.[^322] ئىسرائىل قانۇنى ئاستىدىكى، شەرقىي قۇدس ۋە گولان ئېگىزلىكلىرىنى ئۆز ئىچىگە ئالغان ئومۇمىي كۆلەم 22,072 كىۋادرات كىلومېتىر (8,522 كىۋادرات ميل)،[^323] ھەمدە ھەربىي كونترول قىلىنىدىغان ۋە قىسمەن پەلەستىن باشقۇرىدىغان غەربىي قىرغاق زېمىنىنى ئۆز ئىچىگە ئالغان ئىسرائىل كونترولى ئاستىدىكى ئومۇمىي كۆلەم 27,799 كىۋادرات كىلومېتىر (10,733 كىۋادرات ميل).[^324]

كىچىك كۆلىمىگە قارىماي، ئىسرائىلىيە جەنۇبتىكى [نەگەۋ](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Negev "Negev") چۆلىدىن ئىچكى مۇنبەت [يىزرېئېل ۋادىسىغىچە](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jezreel_Valley "Jezreel Valley") بولغان تۈرلۈك جۇغراپىيەلىك ئالاھىدىلىكلەرگە ئىگە، شىمالدا [جالىلەي](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Galilee "Galilee")، [كەرمەل](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mount_Carmel "Mount Carmel") ۋە [گولان](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Golan_Heights "Golan Heights") تەرەپتىكى تاغ تىزمىلىرى بار. ئوتتۇرا دېڭىز قىرغىقىدىكى [ئىسرائىل دېڭىز بويى تۈزلەڭلىكى](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Israeli_coastal_plain "Israeli coastal plain") نوپۇسنىڭ كۆپ قىسمىنىڭ ماكانىدۇر.[^325] مەركىزىي ئېگىزلىكلەرنىڭ شەرقىدا 6,500 كىلومېتىرلىق (4,039 ميل) [چوڭ يېرىلىش ۋادىسىنىڭ](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Great_Rift_Valley "Great Rift Valley") كىچىك بىر قىسمى بولغان [ئىئوردان يېرىلىش ۋادىسى](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jordan_Rift_Valley "Jordan Rift Valley") ياتىدۇ. [ئىئوردان دەرياسى](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jordan_River "Jordan River") ئىئوردان يېرىلىش ۋادىسى بويىچە، [ھېرمون تېغىدىن](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mount_Hermon "Mount Hermon") [ھۇلا ۋادىسى](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hulah_Valley "Hulah Valley") ۋە [جالىلەي دېڭىزى](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sea_of_Galilee "Sea of Galilee") ئارقىلىق يەر شارى يۈزىنىڭ [ئەڭ تۆۋەن نۇقتىسى](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Extreme_points_of_Earth "Extreme points of Earth") بولغان [ئۆلۈك دېڭىزغىچە](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dead_Sea "Dead Sea") ئېقىدۇ.[^326] تېخىمۇ جەنۇبتا [قىزىل دېڭىزنىڭ](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Red_Sea "Red Sea") بىر قىسمى بولغان [ئېيلات قولتۇقى](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gulf_of_Aqaba "Gulf of Aqaba") بىلەن ئاخىرلىشىدىغان [ئارابا](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Arabah "Arabah") بار. [مەختېش](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Makhtesh "Makhtesh")، ياكى "ئېروزىيە سىركلىرى" نەگەۋ ۋە [سىنا يېرىم ئارىلىغا](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sinai_Peninsula "Sinai Peninsula") خاس بولۇپ، ئەڭ چوڭى 38 km ئۇزۇنلۇقتىكى [مەختېش رامون](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Makhtesh_Ramon "Makhtesh Ramon") دۇر.[^327] ئىسرائىلىيە ئوتتۇرا دېڭىز ھەۋزىسىنىڭ دۆلەتلىرى ئىچىدە ھەر كىۋادرات مېتىرغا ئەڭ كۆپ ئۆسۈملۈك تۈرىگە ئىگە [^328] ۋە تۆت قۇرۇقلۇق ئېكولوگىيىلىك رايوننى ئۆز ئىچىگە ئالىدۇ: [شەرقىي ئوتتۇرا دېڭىز ئىگنە ياپراقلىق-قاتتىق ياپراقلىق-كەڭ ياپراقلىق ئورمانلىرى](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Eastern_Mediterranean_conifer%E2%80%93sclerophyllous%E2%80%93broadleaf_forests "Eastern Mediterranean conifer–sclerophyllous–broadleaf forests")، [جەنۇبىي ئاناتولىيە تاغ ئىگنە ياپراقلىق ۋە يوپۇرماق تاشلايدىغان ئورمانلىرى](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Southern_Anatolian_montane_conifer_and_deciduous_forests "Southern Anatolian montane conifer and deciduous forests")، [ئەرەبىستان چۆلى](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Arabian_Desert "Arabian Desert") ۋە [مېسوپوتامىيە بۇتالىق چۆلى](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mesopotamian_shrub_desert "Mesopotamian shrub desert").[^329] [يەھۇدىي مىللىي فوندى](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jewish_National_Fund "Jewish National Fund") تەرىپىدىن يولغا قويۇلغان چوڭ كۆلەملىك ئورمان تىكىش پروگراممىسىنىڭ نەتىجىسىدە، ئورمانلار 2016-يىلى كۆلەمنىڭ %8.5ىنى تەشكىل قىلدى، 1948-يىلىدىكى %2 دىن ئۆسكەن.[^330] [^331]

### تېكتونىكا ۋە يەر تەۋرەش

ئىئوردان يېرىلىش ۋادىسى [ئۆلۈك دېڭىز يۆتكىلىش](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dead_Sea_Transform "Dead Sea Transform") (ئۆيي) يېرىق سىستېمىسى ئىچىدىكى تېكتونىك ھەرىكەتلەرنىڭ نەتىجىسىدۇر. ئۆيي غەربتە [ئافرىقا تاختىسى](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/African_Plate "African Plate") بىلەن شەرقتە [ئەرەبىستان تاختىسى](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Arabian_Plate "Arabian Plate") ئارىسىدىكى [يۆتكىلىش چېگراسىنى](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Transform_fault "Transform fault") شەكىللەندۈرىدۇ. گولان ئېگىزلىكلىرى ۋە پۈتۈن ئىئوردانىيە ئەرەبىستان تاختىسىنىڭ بىر قىسمى، جالىلەي، غەربىي قىرغاق، دېڭىز بويى تۈزلەڭلىكى ۋە نەگەۋ سىنا يېرىم ئارىلى بىلەن بىرلىكتە ئافرىقا تاختىسىنىڭ ئۈستىدە. بۇ تېكتونىك تەقسىملەش نىسبەتەن يۇقىرى يەر تەۋرەش پائالىيىتىگە ئېلىپ كېلىدۇ. پۈتۈن ئىئوردان ۋادىسى بۆلىكىنىڭ قايتا-قايتا يېرىلغانلىقى ئويلىنىلىدۇ، مەسىلەن [749](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/749_Galilee_earthquake "749 Galilee earthquake") ۋە [1033](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/1033_Jordan_Valley_earthquake "1033 Jordan Valley earthquake") يىللىرىدىكى بۇ قۇرۇلما بويىچە بولغان ئاخىرقى ئىككى چوڭ يەر تەۋرەش جەريانىدا. 1033-يىلدىن بۇيان توپلانغان [سىيرىلىش](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Fault_\(geology\)#Slip,_heave,_throw "Fault (geology)") كەمچىلىكى M <sub>w</sub> ~7.4 لىك بىر يەر تەۋرەشكە سەۋەب بولۇش ئۈچۈن يېتەرلىك.[^332]

ئەڭ ۋەيران قىلغۇچ مەلۇم يەر تەۋرەشلەر مىلادىدىن ئىلگىرى 31-يىلى، [363](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/363_Galilee_earthquake "363 Galilee earthquake")، 749 ۋە 1033-يىللىرى، يەنى ئوتتۇرىچە ھەر تەخمىنەن 400 يىلدا بىر بولغان يۈز بەردى.[^333] ۋەيران قىلغۇچ يەر تەۋرەشلەر تەخمىنەن ھەر 80 يىلدا بىر بولۇپ، جىددىي ھايات يوقىتىشلارغا ئېلىپ كېلىدۇ.[^334] قاتتىق قۇرۇلۇش بەلگىلىمىلىرى ئورنىتىلغان ۋە يېقىندا قۇرۇلغان قۇرۇلمىلار يەر تەۋرەشكە چىداملىق بولسىمۇ، 2007-يىلىغا بارغاندا نۇرغۇن ئاممىۋى بىنالار ۋە 50,000 تۇرالغۇ بىناسى يېڭى ئۆلچەملەرگە ماس كەلمەيتتى ۋە كۈچلۈك بىر يەر تەۋرەشكە دۇچ كەلگەندە "يىمىرىلىپ كېتىشى مۇمكىن" ئىدى.[^334]

### ئىقلىم

![](https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/8/8d/Soil_moisture_and_climate_change.svg/500px-Soil_moisture_and_climate_change.svg.png)

IPCC ئالتىنچى باھالاش دوكلاتىنىڭ پەرەزلىرى ئىقلىم ئۆزگىرىشىنىڭ ھەتتا 2 گرادۇس قىزىش دەرىجىسىدىمۇ ئىسرائىلىيەگە بولغان تەسىرىنى ئېنىق كۆرسىتىدۇ.


تېمپېراتۇرىلار، ئالاھىدە قىش پەسلىدە، كەڭ دائىرىدە پەرقلىنىدۇ. تېل ئاۋىۋ ۋە ھايفا قاتارلىق دېڭىز بويى رايونلىرى سوغۇق، يامغۇرلۇق قىشلار ۋە ئۇزۇن، ئىسسىق يازلار بىلەن ئادەتتىكى [ئوتتۇرا دېڭىز ئىقلىمىغا](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mediterranean_climate "Mediterranean climate") ئىگە. بېئېر شېۋا ۋە شىمالىي نەگەۋ رايونى ئىسسىق يازلار، سوغۇق قىشلار ۋە ئاز يامغۇرلۇق كۈنلەر بىلەن [يېرىم قۇرغاق ئىقلىمغا](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Semi-arid_climate "Semi-arid climate") ئىگە. جەنۇبىي نەگەۋ ۋە ئارابا رايونلىرى ناھايىتى ئىسسىق، قۇرغاق يازلار ۋە ئاز يامغۇرلۇق كۈنلەر بىلەن يۇمشاق قىشلارغا ئىگە [چۆل ئىقلىمىغا](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Desert_climate "Desert climate") ئىگە. ئەڭ يۇقىرى تېمپېراتۇرا بولغان 54 °C (129 °F) 1942-يىلى [تىرات زىۋى](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tirat_Zvi "Tirat Zvi") كىببۇتسىدا خاتىرىلەنگەن.[^335] [^336] تاغلىق رايونلار شاماللىق ۋە سوغۇق بولۇشى مۇمكىن، 750 مېتىر (2,460 fut) ياكى ئۇنىڭدىن يۇقىرى ئېگىزلىكتىكى (قۇدس بىلەن ئوخشاش ئېگىزلىك) رايونلار ئادەتتە ھەر يىلى كەم دېگەندە بىر قېتىم [قار يېغىشىنى](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Snow_in_Israel "Snow in Israel") باشتىن كەچۈرىدۇ.[^337] مايدىن سېنتەبىرگىچە، يامغۇر نادېر.[^338] [^339]

مۆتىدىل ۋە تروپىك زونىلار ئارىسىدىكى ئورنى سەۋەبىدىن، تۆت خىل [پىتوجۇغراپىيىلىك](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Phytogeography "Phytogeography") رايون بار. شۇڭا، ئۆسۈملۈك ۋە ھايۋانات دۇنياسى ئىنتايىن خىلمۇخىل. ئىسرائىلىيەدە 2,867 خىل مەلۇم [ئۆسۈملۈك تۈرى](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_endemic_flora_of_Israel "List of endemic flora of Israel") بار. ئۇلارنىڭ ئىچىدىن كەم دېگەندە 253 خىل [كىرگۈزۈلگەن ۋە يەرلىك ئەمەس](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_adventive_wild_plants_in_Israel "List of adventive wild plants in Israel").[^340] ئىسرائىلىيەدە 380 [تەبىئىي قوغداش رايونى](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/National_parks_and_nature_reserves_of_Israel "National parks and nature reserves of Israel") بار.[^341]

![](https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/e/e1/Water_stress%2C_top_countries_%282022%29.svg/250px-Water_stress%2C_top_countries_%282022%29.svg.png)

ئىسرائىلىيە 2022-يىلى دۇنيادىكى ئون ئۈچىنچى ئەڭ سۇ بېسىمى ئاستىدىكى دۆلەت ئىدى.

ئاز سۇ مەنبەلىرى بىلەن، ئىسرائىلىيە [تېمىپ سۇغىرىش](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Drip_irrigation "Drip irrigation") قاتارلىق تۈرلۈك سۇ تېجەش تېخنىكىلىرىنى يېتىلدۈردى.[^342] [^343] [قۇياش ئېنېرگىيىسى](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Solar_energy "Solar energy") ئۈچۈن بولىدىغان زور مىقداردىكى قۇياش نۇرى [ئىسرائىلىيەنى كىشى بېشىغا قۇياش ئېنېرگىيىسى ئىشلىتىشتە ئالدىنقى دۆلەت](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Solar_power_in_Israel "Solar power in Israel") قىلىدۇ — ئەمەلىيەتتە ھەر بىر ئۆيدە سۇ ئىسسىتىش ئۈچۈن قۇياش پانېللىرى ئىشلىتىلىدۇ.[^344] [مۇھىت قوغداش مىنىستىرلىكى](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ministry_of_Environmental_Protection_\(Israel\) "Ministry of Environmental Protection (Israel)") [ئىقلىم ئۆزگىرىشىنىڭ](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Climate_change_in_Israel "Climate change in Israel") "ھاياتنىڭ بارلىق ساھەلىرىگە ھەل قىلغۇچ تەسىر كۆرسىتىدىغانلىقىنى"، ئالاھىدە ئاجىز نوپۇس گۇرۇھلىرىغا، دوكلات قىلدى.[^345]

[^1]: 20,770 km <sup>2</sup> is Israel within the [Green Line](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Green_Line_\(Israel\) "Green Line (Israel)"). 22,072 km <sup>2</sup> includes the occupied [Golan Heights](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Golan_Heights "Golan Heights") (c. 1,200 km <sup>2</sup> (460 sq mi)) and [East Jerusalem](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/East_Jerusalem "East Jerusalem") (c. 64 km <sup>2</sup> (25 sq mi)).

[^3]: Jerusalem is Israel's largest city if including [East Jerusalem](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/East_Jerusalem "East Jerusalem"), which is widely recognised as occupied territory.[^16] If East Jerusalem is not counted, the largest city would be [Tel Aviv](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tel_Aviv "Tel Aviv").

[^4]: Arabic has a "special status" as set by the [Basic Law of 2018](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Basic_Law:_Israel_as_the_Nation-State_of_the_Jewish_People "Basic Law: Israel as the Nation-State of the Jewish People"), which allows it to be used by official institutions.[^18] [^19] Prior to that law's passage, Arabic had been an official language alongside Hebrew.[^20]

[^5]: Israeli population and economic data covers the economic territory of Israel, including the Golan Heights, East Jerusalem and Israeli settlements in the West Bank.[^370] [^371]

[^35]: Meir-Glitzenstein, Esther (Fall 2018). "Turning Points in the Historiography of Jewish Immigration from Arab Countries to Israel". *Israel Studies*. **23** (3). Indiana University Press: 114–122. [doi](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Doi_\(identifier\) "Doi (identifier)"):[10.2979/israelstudies.23.3.15](https://doi.org/10.2979%2Fisraelstudies.23.3.15). [JSTOR](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/JSTOR_\(identifier\) "JSTOR (identifier)") [10.2979/israelstudies.23.3.15](https://www.jstor.org/stable/10.2979/israelstudies.23.3.15). [S2CID](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/S2CID_\(identifier\) "S2CID (identifier)") [150208821](https://api.semanticscholar.org/CorpusID:150208821). The mass immigration from Arab countries began in mid-1949 and included three communities that relocated to Israel almost in their entirety: 31,000 Jews from Libya, 50,000 from Yemen, and 125,000 from Iraq. Additional immigrants arrived from Egypt, Morocco, Tunisia, Turkey, Iran, India, and elsewhere. Within three years, the Jewish population of Israel doubled. The ethnic composition of the population shifted as well, as immigrants from Muslim counties and their offspring now comprised one third of the Jewish population—an unprecedented phenomenon in global immigration history. From 1952–60, Israel regulated and restricted immigration from Muslim countries with a selective immigration policy based on economic criteria, and sent these immigrants, most of whom were North African, to peripheral Israeli settlements. The selective immigration policy ended in 1961 when, following an agreement between Israel and Morocco, about 100,000 Jews immigrated to the State. From 1952–68 about 600,000 Jews arrived in Israel, three quarters of whom were from Arab countries and the remaining immigrants were largely from Eastern Europe. Today fewer than 30,000 remain in Muslim countries, mostly concentrated in Iran and Turkey.

[^39]: Cleveland, William L.; Bunton, Martin (2016). [*A History of the Modern Middle East*](https://books.google.com/books?id=CUhaDwAAQBAJ&pg=PT290). [ISBN](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/ISBN_\(identifier\) "ISBN (identifier)") [978-0-429-97513-4](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Special:BookSources/978-0-429-97513-4 "Special:BookSources/978-0-429-97513-4"). Not only was there no Palestinian Arab state, but the vast majority of the Arab population in the territory that became Israel-over 700,000 people-had become refugees. The Arab flight from Palestine began during the intercommunal war and was at first the normal reaction of a civilian population to nearby fighting-a temporary evacuation from the zone of combat with plans to return once hostilities ceased. However, during spring and early summer 1948, the flight of the Palestinian Arabs was transformed into a permanent mass exodus....

The Commission has been investigating the events on and since 7 October 2023 for the last two years, and concluded that Israeli authorities and Israeli security forces committed four of the five genocidal acts defined by the 1948 Convention on the Prevention and Punishment of the Crime of Genocide, namely killing, causing serious bodily or mental harm, deliberately inflicting conditions of life calculated to bring about the destruction of the Palestinians in whole or in part, and imposing measures intended to prevent births.

Retrieved 22 December 2025. Para. 233: the Commission concludes that the 'direct and public incitement to commit genocide' under article III(c) of the Genocide Convention is established. Para. 252. The Commission concludes on reasonable grounds that the Israeli authorities and Israeli security forces have committed and are continuing to commit the following actus reus of genocide against the Palestinians in the Gaza Strip, namely (i) killing members of the group; (ii) causing serious bodily or mental harm to members of the group; (iii) deliberately inflicting on the group conditions of life calculated to bring about its physical destruction in whole or in part; and (iv) imposing measures intended to prevent births within the group
- [""More than a human can bear": Israel's systematic use of sexual, reproductive and other forms of gender-based violence since 7 October 2023"](https://www.ohchr.org/sites/default/files/documents/hrbodies/hrcouncil/sessions-regular/session58/a-hrc-58-crp-6.pdf) (PDF). *UN Human Rights Council*. 13 March 2025. Retrieved 22 December 2025. Para. 175: The Commission concludes that the destruction of the Basma IVF clinic was a measure intended to prevent births among Palestinians in Gaza, which is a genocidal act under the Rome Statute and Genocide Convention. Para. 178: The Commission concludes that the ISF caused serious bodily and mental harm to members of this group, and deliberately inflicted conditions of life that were calculated to bring about the physical destruction of Palestinians in Gaza as a group, in whole or in part, which are categories of genocidal acts in the Rome Statute and the Genocide Convention. Para. 218: The Commission finds that the Israeli authorities have destroyed in part the reproductive capacity of the Palestinians in Gaza as a group, including by imposing measures intended to prevent births, one of the categories of genocidal acts in the Rome Statute and the Genocide Convention. Para. 219: The harm for pregnant, lactating and new mothers is of an unprecedented scale in Gaza…The underlying acts amount to crimes against humanity and deliberately inflicting conditions of life calculated to bring about the physical destruction of Palestinians as a group, one of the categories of genocidal acts in the Rome Statute and the Genocide Convention
- ["UN Independent International Commission of Inquiry, legal analysis and recommendations on the implementation of the International Court of Justice Advisory Opinion, Legal Consequences arising from the Policies and Practices of Israel in the Occupied Palestinian Territory"](https://www.ohchr.org/sites/default/files/documents/hrbodies/hrcouncil/coiopt/2024-10-18-COI-position-paper_co-israel.pdf) (PDF). *UN Human Rights Council*. 18 October 2024. Retrieved 22 December 2025. Para. 23: On the issue of genocide, the Commission notes the provisional measures orders issued by the Court in the Application of the Convention on the Prevention and Punishment of the Crime of Genocide in the Gaza Strip (South Africa v. Israel). The Commission finds that all States are on notice that Israel may be or is committing internationally wrongful acts in both its conduct in the military operations in Gaza and its unlawful occupation of the West Bank, including East Jerusalem. Thus, the Commission finds that, unless States cease their aid and assistance to Israel in the commission of these acts, those States shall be deemed to be complicit in those internationally wrongful acts.
- ["Application of the Genocide Convention (Order 26 I 24)"](https://www.icj-cij.org/sites/default/files/case-related/192/192-20240126-ord-01-00-en.pdf) (PDF). *International Court of Justice*. 26 January 2024. [Archived](https://web.archive.org/web/20250926134400/https://www.un.org/unispal/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/192-20240126-ord-01-00-en-1.pdf) (PDF) from the original on 26 September 2025. - ["Application of the Genocide Convention (Order 24 V 24)"](https://www.icj-cij.org/sites/default/files/case-related/192/192-20240524-ord-01-00-en.pdf) (PDF). *International Court of Justice*. 24 May 2025. As a West Semitic personal name it existed long before it became a tribal or a geographical name. This is not without significance, though is it rarely mentioned. We learn of a maryanu named ysr"il (\*Yi¡sr—a"ilu) from Ugarit living in the same period, but the name was already used a thousand years before in Ebla. The word Israel originated as a West Semitic personal name. One of the many names that developed into the name of the ancestor of a clan, of a tribe and finally of a people and a nation.

[^104]: Raviv, Dvir; Ben David, Chaim (2021). ["Cassius Dio's figures for the demographic consequences of the Bar Kokhba War: Exaggeration or reliable account?"](https://doi.org/10.1017%2FS1047759421000271). *Journal of Roman Archaeology*. **34** (2): 585–607. [doi](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Doi_\(identifier\) "Doi (identifier)"):[10.1017/S1047759421000271](https://doi.org/10.1017%2FS1047759421000271). [ISSN](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/ISSN_\(identifier\) "ISSN (identifier)") [1047-7594](https://search.worldcat.org/issn/1047-7594). [S2CID](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/S2CID_\(identifier\) "S2CID (identifier)") [245512193](https://api.semanticscholar.org/CorpusID:245512193). Scholars have long doubted the historical accuracy of Cassius Dio's account of the consequences of the Bar Kokhba War (Roman History 69.14). According to this text, considered the most reliable literary source for the Second Jewish Revolt, the war encompassed all of Judea: the Romans destroyed 985 villages and 50 fortresses, and killed 580,000 rebels. This article reassesses Cassius Dio's figures by drawing on new evidence from excavations and surveys in Judea, Transjordan, and the Galilee. Three research methods are combined: an ethno-archaeological comparison with the settlement picture in the Ottoman Period, comparison with similar settlement studies in the Galilee, and an evaluation of settled sites from the Middle Roman Period (70–136 CE). The study demonstrates the potential contribution of the archaeological record to this issue and supports the view of Cassius Dio's demographic data as a reliable account, which he based on contemporaneous documentation.

[^105]: Mor, Menahem (18 April 2016). *The Second Jewish Revolt*. BRILL. pp. 483–484. [doi](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Doi_\(identifier\) "Doi (identifier)"):[10.1163/9789004314634](https://doi.org/10.1163%2F9789004314634). Land confiscation in Judaea was part of the suppression of the revolt policy of the Romans and punishment for the rebels. But the very claim that the sikarikon laws were annulled for settlement purposes seems to indicate that Jews continued to reside in Judaea even after the Second Revolt. There is no doubt that this area suffered the severest damage from the suppression of the revolt. Settlements in Judaea, such as Herodion and Bethar, had already been destroyed during the course of the revolt, and Jews were expelled from the districts of Gophna, Herodion, and Aqraba. However, it should not be claimed that the region of Judaea was completely destroyed. Jews continued to live in areas such as Lod (Lydda), south of the Hebron Mountain, and the coastal regions. In other areas of the Land of Israel that did not have any direct connection with the Second Revolt, no settlement changes can be identified as resulting from it.

[OCLC](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/OCLC_\(identifier\) "OCLC (identifier)") [1302180905](https://search.worldcat.org/oclc/1302180905). The Jewish community strove to recover from the catastrophic results of the Bar Kokhva revolt (132–135 CE). Although some of these attempts were relatively successful, the Jews never fully recovered. During the Late Roman and Byzantine periods, many Jews emigrated to thriving centres in the diaspora, especially Iraq, whereas some converted to Christianity and others continued to live in the Holy Land, especially in Galilee and the coastal plain. During the Byzantine period, the three provinces of Palestine included more than thirty cities, namely, settlements with a bishop see. After the Muslim conquest in the 630s, most of these cities declined and eventually disappeared. As a result, in many cases the local ecclesiastical administration weakened, while in others it simply ceased to exist. Consequently, many local Christians converted to Islam. Thus, almost twelve centuries later, when the army led by Napoleon Bonaparte arrived in the Holy Land, most of the local population was Muslim.

The dominant view of the history of Palestine during the Byzantine period links the early phases of the consecration of the land during the fourth century and the substantial external financial investment that accompanied the building of churches on holy sites on the one hand with the Christianisation of the population on the other. Churches were erected primarily at the holy sites, 12 while at the same time Palestine's position and unique status as the Christian 'Holy Land' became more firmly rooted. All this, coupled with immigration and conversion, allegedly meant that the Christianisation of Palestine took place much more rapidly than that of other areas of the Roman empire, brought in its wake the annihilation of the pagan cults and meant that by the middle of the fifth century there was a clear Christian majority.

From the data given above it can be concluded that the Muslim population of Central Samaria, during the early Muslim period, was not an autochthonous population which had converted to Christianity. They arrived there either by way of migration or as a result of a process of sedentarization of the nomads who had filled the vacuum created by the departing Samaritans at the end of the Byzantine period \[...\] To sum up: in the only rural region in Palestine in which, according to all the written and archeological sources, the process of Islamization was completed already in the twelfth century, there occurred events consistent with the model propounded by Levtzion and Vryonis: the region was abandoned by its original sedentary population and the vacuum was apparently filled by nomads who, at a later stage, gradually became sedentarized

Another major cause of antagonism was the labor controversy. The hard core of Second Aliyah socialists, who were to become the Yishuv's leaders in the 1920s and 1930s, believed that the settler economy must not depend on or exploit Arab labor... But, in reality, rather than "meshing," the nationalist ethos had simply overpowered and driven out the socialist ethos... There were other reasons for the "conquest of labor." The socialists of the Second Aliyah used the term to denote three things: overcoming the Jews' traditional remove from agricultural labor and helping them transform into the "new Jews"; struggling against employers for better conditions; and replacing Arabs with Jews in manual jobs.

Moreover, the quality of the land granted to the proposed Jewish state was highly skewed in its favour. UNSCOP reported that under its majority plan "\[t\]he Jews will have the more economically developed part of the country embracing practically the whole of the citrus-producing area"—Palestine's staple export crop—even though approximately half of the citrus-bearing land was owned by the Arabs. In addition, according to updated British records submitted to the ad hoc committee's two sub-committees, "of the irrigated, cultivable areas" of the country, 84 per cent would be in the Jewish State and 16 per cent would be in the Arab State"."

The blockade closed Israel's sea lane to East Africa and the Far East, hindering the development of Israel's southern port of Eilat and its hinterland, the Nege. Another important objective of the Israeli war plan was the elimination of the terrorist bases in the Gaza Strip, from which daily fedayeen incursions into Israel made life unbearable for its southern population. And last but not least, the concentration of the Egyptian forces in the Sinai Peninsula, armed with the newly acquired weapons from the Soviet bloc, prepared for an attack on Israel. Here, Ben-Gurion believed, was a time bomb that had to be defused before it was too late. Reaching the Suez Canal did not figure at all in Israel's war objectives.

The escalation continued with the Egyptian blockade of the Straits of Tiran, and Nasser's nationalization of the Suez Canal in July 1956. On October 14, Nasser made clear his intent:"I am not solely fighting against Israel itself. My task is to deliver the Arab world from destruction through Israel's intrigue, which has its roots abroad. Our hatred is very strong. There is no sense in talking about peace with Israel. There is not even the smallest place for negotiations." Less than two weeks later, on October 25, Egypt signed a tripartite agreement with Syria and Jordan placing Nasser in command of all three armies. The continued blockade of the Suez Canal and Gulf of Aqaba to Israeli shipping, combined with the increased fedayeen attacks and the bellicosity of recent Arab statements, prompted Israel, with the backing of Britain and France, to attack Egypt on October 29, 1956.

- [Amnesty International](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Amnesty_International "Amnesty International") (2024). [Archived](https://web.archive.org/web/20241205121850/https://amnesty.ca/wp-content/uploads/2024/12/Amnesty-International-Gaza-Genocide-Report-December-4-2024.pdf) (PDF) from the original on 5 December 2024. This report focuses on the Israeli authorities' policies and actions in Gaza as part of the military offensive they launched in the wake of the Hamas-led attacks on 7 October 2023 while situating them within the broader context of Israel's unlawful occupation, and system of apartheid against Palestinians in Gaza, the West Bank, including East Jerusalem, and Israel. It assesses allegations of violations and crimes under international law by Israel in Gaza within the framework of genocide under international law, concluding that there is sufficient evidence to believe that Israel's conduct in Gaza following 7 October 2023 amounts to genocide.

- [B'Tselem 2025](#CITEREFB'Tselem2025), p. 86: "The review presented in this report leaves no room for doubt: since October 2023, the Israeli regime has been responsible for carrying out genocide against the Palestinians in the Gaza Strip. [^356]: Charbit, Denis (2014). ["Israel's Self-Restrained Secularism from the 1947 Status Quo Letter to the Present"](https://books.google.com/books?id=gThvBAAAQBAJ&pg=PAPA167). In Berlinerblau, Jacques; Fainberg, Sarah; Nou, Aurora (eds.). *Secularism on the Edge: Rethinking Church-State Relations in the United States, France, and Israel*. Palgrave Macmillan. pp. 167–169. [ISBN](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/ISBN_\(identifier\) "ISBN (identifier)") [978-1-137-38115-6](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Special:BookSources/978-1-137-38115-6 "Special:BookSources/978-1-137-38115-6"). The compromise, therefore, was to choose constructive ambiguity: as surprising as it may seem, there is no law that declares Judaism the official religion of Israel. However, there is no other law that declares Israel's neutrality toward all confessions. Judaism is not recognized as the official religion of the state, and even though the Jewish, Muslim and Christian clergy receive their salaries from the state, this fact does not make Israel a neutral state. This apparent pluralism cannot dissimulate the fact that Israel displays a clear and undoubtedly hierarchical pluralism in religious matters.... It is important to note that from a multicultural point of view, this self-restrained secularism allows Muslim law to be practiced in Israel for personal matters of the Muslim community. As surprising as it seems, if not paradoxical for a state in war, Israel is the only Western democratic country in which Sharia enjoys such an official status.

[^357]: Sharot, Stephen (2007). ["Judaism in Israel: Public Religion, Neo-Traditionalism, Messianism, and Ethno-Religious Conflict"](https://books.google.com/books?id=vA8edg7bv0kC&pg=PAPA671). In Beckford, James A.; Demerath, Jay (eds.). *The Sage Handbook of the Sociology of Religion*. Sage Publications. pp. 671–672. [ISBN](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/ISBN_\(identifier\) "ISBN (identifier)") [978-1-4129-1195-5](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Special:BookSources/978-1-4129-1195-5 "Special:BookSources/978-1-4129-1195-5"). It is true that Jewish Israelis, and secular Israelis in particular, conceive of religion as shaped by a state-sponsored religious establishment. There is no formal state religion in Israel, but the state gives its official recognition and financial support to particular religious communities, Jewish, Islamic and Christian, whose religious authorities and courts are empowered to deal with matters of personal status and family law, such as marriage, divorce, and alimony, that are binding on all members of the communities.

[^358]: Jacoby, Tami Amanda (2005). [*Women in Zones of Conflict: Power and Resistance in Israel*](https://books.google.com/books?id=pr1LJNrlmuIC&pg=PAPA53). McGill-Queen's University Press. pp. 53–54. [ISBN](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/ISBN_\(identifier\) "ISBN (identifier)") [978-0-7735-2993-9](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Special:BookSources/978-0-7735-2993-9 "Special:BookSources/978-0-7735-2993-9"). Although there is no official religion in Israel, there is also no clear separation between religion and state. In Israeli public life, tensions frequently arise among different streams of Judaism: Ultra-Orthodox, National-Religious, *Mesorati* (Conservative), Reconstructionist Progressive (Reform), and varying combinations of traditionalism and non-observance. Despite this variety in religious observances in society, Orthodox Judaism prevails institutionally over the other streams. This boundary is an historical consequence of the unique evolution of the relationship between Israel nationalism and state building.... Since the founding period, in order to defuse religious tensions, the State of Israel has adopted what is known as the 'status quo,' an unwritten agreement stipulating that no further changes would be made in the status of religion, and that conflict between the observant and non-observant sectors would be handled circumstantially. The 'status quo' has since pertained to the legal status of both religious and secular Jews in Israel. This situation was designed to appease the religious sector, and has been upheld indefinitely through the disproportionate power of religious political parties in all subsequent coalition governments.... On one hand, the Declaration of Independence adopted in 1948 explicitly guarantees freedom of religion. On the other, it simultaneously prevents the separation of religion and state in Israel.

It is now clear that Israel is a true democracy in its broadest sense only for its Jewish citizens. The Arab-Israeli (or, as some prefer, the Palestinian-Israeli) peoples, roughly 20 per cent of the total population of Israel its pre-1967 boundaries, are citizens and have voting rights, but they face political, economic, and social discrimination. And, of course, Israeli democracy is inapplicable to the nearly 4 million Palestinian Arabs in the West Bank and Gaza, conquered by Israel in June 1967, who are occupied, repressed, and in many ways, directly and indirectly, effectively ruled by Israel.

[doi](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Doi_\(identifier\) "Doi (identifier)"):[10.1093/icon/mol021](https://doi.org/10.1093%2Ficon%2Fmol021). The real controversy hovering over all the litigation on the security barrier concerns the fate of the Israeli settlements in the occupied territories. Since 1967, Israel has allowed and even encouraged its citizens to live in the new settlements established in the territories, motivated by religious and national sentiments attached to the history of the Jewish nation in the land of Israel. This policy has also been justified in terms of security interests, taking into consideration the dangerous geographic circumstances of Israel before 1967 (where Israeli areas on the Mediterranean coast were potentially threatened by Jordanian control of the West Bank ridge). The international community, for its part, has viewed this policy as patently illegal, based on the provisions of the Fourth Geneva Convention that prohibit moving populations to or from territories under occupation.

[Konrad Adenauer Foundation](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Konrad_Adenauer_Foundation "Konrad Adenauer Foundation"). December 2017. pp. 22, 25, 28. (p.28) "The positions of the participants in the focus groups reflect the strength of Palestinian-Arab identity among Arab citizens and the fact that they do not see a contradiction between Palestinian-Arab national identity and Israeli civic identity. The designation "Israeli-Arab" aroused great opposition in the focus groups, as did Israel's Independence Day.... The collective position presented in the focus group discussions finds expression in the public sphere and emphasizes the Palestinian national identity. Conversely, the responses of the survey participants reveal individual attitudes that assign a broader (albeit secondary, identity) dimension to the component of Israeli civic identity"; quote (p.25): The designation "Arab citizens of Israel" was acceptable to them on the basis of the understanding that it is impossible to live without citizenship, and as long as Israeli citizenship does not harm the national consciousness. Conversely, the participants spoke out against the designation "Arab-Israeli"...
